MA	<p>0151_d1e8</p> <p>Restriction enzymes are proteins that cut DNA at specific sequences. <span style="color: #00775f;"><i>TaiI</i></span> is derived from Thermus aquaticus cs1-331, and <span style="color: #0067cc;"><i>MroI</i></span> is derived from Micrococcus roseus. Both enzymes are labeled on the map.</p><p><strong>DNA Fragment Question:</strong> Shown below is a short DNA fragment that is only 10 kb in length.
